### Question 3 settlement and question 7 observation record (2026-11-05)

**Question 3 is closed for the first bounded-trial specification.** A declared known ordinary encoding may use a versioned counter for newly charged text. On an unknown-encoding route outside an enrolled trial, full-local governs. For eligible new text inside the trial, run both pinned split rules with the shared o200k ranks on the identical decoded projection, sum each count over 8,192-character chunks, take the larger total and add ceil(6% of that total) once. Plain o200k plus 6% stays in shadow. Weighted Han rates and spacing surcharges remain offline comparisons. The tested rate and image allowance retain their provisional scope. The fixed/full-local, output, fallback and trial stop rules retain their separate meanings. This settles the trial formula, not routine authority, calibration of an unknown upstream, implementation or enrollment.

**Comparable margins.** The earlier ten blocks covered 66 pages. The next sixteen covered the other 107 pages under the predeclared grouping. On raw bodies, the minimum cross-split margins were +1 and +7 respectively. On the filled-minus-empty tool field they were +11 and +10. On the complete template text increment relative to the predecessor they were +438 and +437. The flat 34% holdout shortfall of eight was on the tool-field increment. This is a prospective page holdout within one book, OCR engine and target encoding. Its observed minima are not designed surplus levels or upper bounds. Stops and budgets limit further trial exposure only once the relevant condition is observed. They cannot prevent the first miss or confirm that a route's usage is accurate. On policy pages 2 and 3, matching the Kimi split rule but using o200k ranks still gave 379 versus 408 and 773 versus 821 ordinary Kimi tokens. The six- then 51- and 505-token field misses in the repeated-page control exclude interpreting this residual as a small fixed overhead. No new rate follows from repeats. All 28 eligible historical pairs had zero extra reservation and zero fit changes. Running another count can still cost CPU time; its latency was not measured. [Matched results](#question-3-cross-split-trial-candidate-2026-11-05)

**Question 7 record proposal.** One logical preparation opportunity can have multiple candidate payloads or no physical attempt. Keep each immutable candidate hash and its Pf/Pa form under the preparation ID. Link the decision/disposition, actual request and every physical attempt to that chain. Restart or preview of an existing decision is not another opportunity. A materially changed candidate following a deferral or override needs a linked evaluation. Report first requests, ineligible anchors, unavailable evidence, off/suspended trials and withheld candidates in the opportunity denominator with their reasons. Eligible comparisons, physical exposures, attributable responses and completed tasks have their own denominators.

| When | Fields and interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| At preparation on each exact candidate | Governing method (full-local or anchored) and version, anchor basis and eligibility, trial mode, disposition and fit flags. Pf/Pa identifies a rendered payload, not a third estimator. Keep both split counts and their asset/recipe identities, plain and governing reservations, output and allowance, extra tokens reserved, remaining headroom and same-byte fit disagreement. A trial-rendered send where both admit its final bytes remains identifiable. A comparison that was not computed has a reason and no numerical value. |
| Text diagnostics | On the same decoded, newly charged sequence before chunking, record total Unicode code points, absolute script counts (Han, Hiragana, Katakana and Hangul separately), and their shares with that denominator. Record the count of literal U+0020 spaces in runs immediately bounded by Han characters; count spaces, not just runs. Include absolute text volume and input representation. Define a zero-length share as unavailable. Features help stratify observations; the actual split counts establish the reserve difference. Store scalar diagnostics outside model-visible shadow feedback. |
| At dispatch and settlement | Record the actual frozen request, attempt, outcome and attributable normalized input where available. For that candidate, define signed reservation error as reserved input minus reported input. With a compatible accepted predecessor one may also compare the priced increment against the difference of reported inputs, recording control, image and policy changes. A failed or unsent candidate has no matched usage by default. A later compacted or deferred request cannot supply its missing outcome. |
| Offline comparison versus product result | Filled-minus-empty rendered field error requires a controlled comparison or genuinely attributable component evidence. Normal provider input totals cannot supply that number per tool field. Keep offline template results distinct from live usage. Record veto, override, deferral, compaction attempt, accepted handoff, fidelity, interruption and whole-task cost at the stage that actually establishes each fact. |

**Consolidation before implementation.** After question 7 validation/acceptance and question 8 route-counting discussion are settled, consolidate adopted rules in a versioned usage-anchoring section of the existing [context management and capacity contract](../design/CONTEXT_MANAGEMENT.md#assembled-request-budget). The [contract navigator](../CONTRACTS.md) already assigns capacity there. Preserve the deployed rule and its edition, label specification versus implementation status, and cite the relevant dated discussion for each rule family: eligibility and supported edits; zero-credit release; authority, order, budget and suspension; freeze, reopen and retry; text/image/output pricing; observations and acceptance. Link to coordination and runtime owners where their existing contracts govern; avoid a second normative specification. Leave rejected alternatives and the experiment chronology here. Question 8 remains last, including direct Chat Completions support. Neither design consolidation nor runtime implementation was performed in this review.

The offline audit in `.local/trial-record-audit-20261105-07412a04/` recomputes the stage-specific minima from the per-block rows and checks the 28 historical pairs against the script diagnostics. No provider or task operations were performed.

### Question 7 promotion evidence and exposure (2026-11-05)

**Review disposition.** Predeclare evidence, margin, diversity, coverage, operating safeguards and product criteria before enrollment. All six matter to a routine-authority decision. The first bounded trial can close inconclusively. Its exhaustion or clean stop never resets its budget or activates routine mode. The former 0.33 s / two-million-character benchmark was reviewer-reported on base64-like input. Its cache state, cold-start coverage and relevance to the dual-pass admission path were not established.

**Denominators and uncertainty.** Start the exposure ledger at physical attempt start. Keep failed, ambiguous and missing-usage attempts visible. An accepted anchor-only send with normalized attributable input on the exact final payload contributes a scored reservation margin. Counting only these successes for a failure rate would hide the failures by construction. A permitted retry is additional exposure for the same frozen request, not independent support. Trial-rendered both-fit sends consume budget but cannot fill the minimum for same-byte anchor-only evidence. A veto, including a C override, is a trial disposition with its own linked outcome. Report withheld opportunities, exposures by task/source, distinct logical candidates and failures, and scored successes separately. The 34 historical sends contained 28 eligible pairs, two conversions of one PDF and zero anchor-only sends. They cannot contribute 28 successes toward promotion.

For a fixed sample of independent attempts with a common failure probability, complete observation and zero failures, the one-sided 95% upper limit is 1 minus 0.05 to the power 1/n. At n=30 this is 9.50%. Illustratively, upper limits of 5% or 1% require at least 59 or 299 independent zero-failure observations respectively. These are feasibility illustrations only. The first-veto suspension, other early stops, related requests and source/route clustering constrain interpretation. A per-conversion cap limits dominance but does not confer independence. Set minimum scored opportunities, distinct conversions and distinct source families, maximum concentration, a total attempt ceiling and a closing horizon together. If the minimum cannot fit the budget, or cannot be attained when the trial closes, declare insufficient evidence. Do not pool across unreviewed configuration changes.

**Margins and product consequences.** On a scored send record signed margin R−U (reservation less attributable input), local dispatch headroom A−R and observed configured headroom A−U, where A is configured input allowance. Dispatch headroom plus signed margin gives observed configured headroom: (A−R)+(R−U)=A−U. None measures the upstream maximum. Show minima, lower tail and task/source breakdown together with the new-text charge, image changes and proximity to the boundary. Predeclare any floor and treatment of tiny/zero denominators. Larger reserves can improve margin and simultaneously eliminate useful admissions, so a floor alone rewards overreservation. A reported undercount may occur on an accepted request with room left.

A same-byte fit disagreement is direct estimator evidence. The particular content full-local would defer or the next action it would select requires a read-only, exact-state comparison through the applicable admission/continuation path, including the original Pf baseline where Pa differs. If the comparison was not run or reproducible, record the action as unknown. Full-local can wait or stop as well as defer or select compaction. A local compaction selection is not a counterfactual dispatch or an accepted handoff. Preserve exact retained content or references, actual veto dispositions, interruptions and compaction attempts separately; do not net veto counts against projected triggers as if they had equal cost. Source-led saved/reopened/exported checks can establish that the observed conversions met their acceptance criteria. Without a comparable control they cannot establish unchanged fidelity relative to another method. Report whole-task fees only when known. A meaningful first-trial product claim can be that the agent received useful content the reproduced local alternative would have deferred and completed an acceptable delivery; stronger comparative claims need corresponding evidence.

**Stop, attribution and authority.** A reservation exceedance, non-accepted or unresolved decisive attempt, or confirmed overflow invokes the appropriate stop and review. A non-accepted HTTP send with an unrelated or unknown cause prevents a clean pass; it does not diagnose overflow or establish an estimator defect. Conversely, a clean suspension on a C veto is not itself an estimator failure. Missing attribution leaves accuracy unscored. A trial may fail a predeclared acceptance criterion, or close inconclusively with insufficient opportunity, diversity, attribution, coverage or product evidence. Promotion requires explicit review of the exact route/configuration and rule versions, evidence across all six criteria, and a documented operating policy for the achieved scope. Carry-over after a change requires its own decision.

The proposed routine mode would remove both the cumulative budget and the concurrency slot. The trial with one outstanding decisive attempt cannot establish how many failures could occur concurrently before a suspension takes effect. A per-task guard is reactive. A route with no trusted overflow signal cannot promise automatic suspension on confirmed overflow; a failure associated with an anchor-only send is a distinct observable, and any precautionary suspension rule must be specified independently. Preserving shadow records supplies observability but cannot prospectively protect unobserved workloads. Recommendation for discussion: make a clean first trial eligible for review of a separately authorized, still-bounded expansion with an explicit concurrency limit. Routine authority, especially unrestricted concurrency, needs its own risk decision. Do not assume more exposure is necessary or authorized after an inconclusive result. No numeric promotion floor, sample size, enrollment or wider exposure is selected here. [Lifecycle and authority](#question-6-trial-scope-exhaustion-and-lineage-2026-11-04), [observation record](#question-3-settlement-and-question-7-observation-record-2026-11-05), [continuation alternatives](../../src/pdfmd/context_updates.py).

### Question 7 bounded expansion and two-sided acceptance (2026-11-05)

**Scope of the first decision.** A clean first trial can, at most, earn review for a separately authorized, still-bounded expansion of the same versioned route/profile, with all safeguards retained. Keep the one-slot cap by default. Enlarging the cumulative budget, raising simultaneous exposure, changing the estimator and granting routine authority are distinct decisions. Promotion here means eligibility for the specified expansion. No automatic next trial, larger cap or routine mode follows. Numeric thresholds remain open.

**Two-sided acceptance.** Require evidence of both adequate safety margins and useful capacity recovery. For the identical finalized payload and allowance A, let F and R denote the full-local and anchored input reservations and U the attributable reported input. Record margin M = R − U and recovered local headroom G = F − R, alongside F − A (full-local shortfall when positive) and A − R (remaining local headroom under the anchor). On a scored candidate M + G = F − U. Moving one token from reservation to recovered headroom reduces the margin by exactly one. On an anchor-only admission G is positive by definition. Report signed G on all comparable eligible candidates, including unfavorable differences, and separately on the decisive subset. Missing usage prevents scoring M; it does not prevent computing same-byte G.

An offline join of the saved cross-split trial headroom against the invariant-core **decoded full-flat** comparator on all 28 eligible historical sends gave G from 2,429 to 51,404 (median 19,030.5 tokens). All 28 already fit full-local, which had at least 2,625 tokens left. Consequently the large median changed zero local fit decisions. Conversely, in a synthetic boundary case with A=1,000, F=1,001 and R=999, two recovered tokens change fit. Set any minimum recovered-headroom statistic before enrollment, with its population, scale and reason. Use it alongside an evidenced change in admission or useful content retained near the boundary. An increase in G from inflating the comparator, or improvement from a handful of arbitrarily large requests, must not earn promotion. Freeze the comparator and report per-task/source distributions and coverage. A predeclared alternative based on a share of informative opportunities with material changes may better represent the intended product benefit than an arbitrary token median. Safety, opportunity/spread and source-led delivery criteria remain independent gates.

**Concurrency and contention.** Arithmetic on fixed bytes, frozen basis and accounting generation is independent of overlapping dispatches. Revocation, slot availability and which basis is visible at selection can still change the decision. The exposure limit K bounds outstanding authorized attempts at a time, subject to atomic reservation, durable stop and retry checks. It does not bound cumulative attempts over a trial. Record otherwise dispatchable opportunities needing a slot (including trial-rendered both-fit), busy refusals, distinct competing tasks and local fallback disposition. Deduplicate re-entry for the opportunity rate; count physical retries separately for exposure. Report busy refusals divided by otherwise slot-qualified opportunities while the trial was active and within budget. Give zero denominator as unavailable. No waiting is introduced. Frequent contention shows the cap binds; an unsent candidate has no success/usage outcome. Low contention describes only the observed task overlap. Use measured contention and actual fallback disruption to assess demand; select K from tolerated simultaneous exposure. If a future trigger requires repeated signals, distinguish its threshold from the outstanding attempts at suspension.

A non-accepted attempt on a final anchor-only candidate with F>A is an observable failure associated with the admission difference. The F>A condition is already part of that cohort. Preserve its HTTP/stream/recovery status and exact route generation. For the trial the first such failure already stops further trial exposure pending attribution. A future precautionary route suspension must define trigger timing, treatment of unresolved/reconciled attempts, deduplication, scope, reset and in-flight dispositions and be tested across tasks and restart. It can work without reading an HTTP error body. Calling it confirmed overflow would be unsupported. Even a specified and tested trigger would not by itself grant routine authority.

**Observable product chain.** Distinguish frozen, actually dispatched and locally accepted exchanges. Verify the specific result/ref, body or range, or native pixels present in the dispatched bytes; the mere presence of a reference is not evidence that its body was available inline. On the linked accepted response, distinguish a proposed call from a successfully executed/reconciled operation or saved edit. Predeclare the association rule, preferably an exact ref or identifiable content plus page range. Mark page-only overlap as weaker and absence of a same-response link as unobserved. A later linked action can be reported separately, without attributing causation. Check the corresponding source pages against saved, reopened and exported output, including the relevant limitations. A reproducible full-local counterfactual plus this observed chain is stronger evidence of useful admission than a recovered-token total. It cannot prove the agent relied on one particular input or that the alternative conversion would have been worse. [Runtime tool reconciliation](../../src/pdfmd/runtime.py) and [earlier promotion evidence](#question-7-promotion-evidence-and-exposure-2026-11-05).

**Next decision.** Predeclare the bounded expansion being considered and the maximum acceptable first-trial exposure. Choose the minimum scored decisive sends, breadth and concentration of sources/conversions, margin and efficiency criteria, physical budget, and time/task closing point as one feasible set before enrollment. Classify the resulting trial as eligible for scoped expansion, rejected on an established criterion, or inconclusive. A stop for an unclassified fault prevents a clean pass and still requires attribution. The two closed historical conversions cannot set these thresholds.

### Question 7 first-trial numbers under review (2026-11-05)

**Feedback exposure.** A shadow number on ordinary Pf agreement is outside the model input and cannot influence that turn through its display. There is one important exception to the reviewer's statement about non-decisive sends. If only the anchor admits Pf, the trial can build Pa and send it with anchored feedback even when both estimates admit Pa. The saved decision fixture has allowance 7,168; Pf estimates 7,169 full-local and 7,159 anchored; Pa estimates 7,163 and 7,154 respectively. It consumes one slot and budget unit, exposes anchored feedback, and contributes zero same-byte decisive observations. Record the feedback actually delivered, the content admitted and the linked action separately. On exposed turns the trial can observe behavior, but admission, payload and displayed headroom change together. It cannot identify the causal effect of the display. A later policy that changes feedback on ordinary Pf agreement is a new intervention. A hold or override can also affect progress even without a decisive success. [Decision order](#question-7-decision-order-shadow-and-retry-2026-11-04)

**Historical feasibility.** Run A had 27 sends, including two compactions, not 27 measured pre-deferral opportunities. The normal projections at the two historical pressure points have no frozen payloads or verified structural eligibility. The 34 frozen sends changed zero fit decisions under the comparisons studied. A yield of zero to three decisive opportunities per long conversion is a planning scenario only. The actual yield remains unknown. Do not select tasks after observing a disagreement merely to fill the quota.

**Recommended package for discussion, not enrollment authority.**

| Parameter | Recommended interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| Exposure and stop | Keep one outstanding trial-funded physical attempt per enrolled route lineage. Cap the total at 20, including Pa both-fit sends and each permitted retry. C overrides use ordinary full-local dispatch accounting. Stop on the first specified adverse outcome; the first governing veto also suspends the trial even if C succeeds. A pause or unclassified fault is a review disposition, not automatic proof of estimator failure. |
| Closing | Start the 60-day clock at trial activation. Enroll at most ten distinct conversion IDs, including conversions with zero opportunities or early termination. After the tenth enrollment prohibit an eleventh, but allow the enrolled cohort to finish within the remaining limits. Close new exposure at budget exhaustion, at cohort closure after the tenth enrollment, or at the 60-day deadline, whichever occurs first, subject to earlier stop/suspension. Reconcile outstanding attempts, and finish fidelity review separately. No new exposure while waiting for evidence, silent reset, or automatic early pass on the tenth score. |
| Score and spread | Require at least 10 distinct final-payload anchor-only logical requests accepted with attributable input, drawn from at least four distinct scored source documents. Preserve the full physical exposure denominator. Reopens, retries and unscored conversions do not increase the minimum. Limit a single conversion to 40% of all distinct scores. Recommend also limiting a single source family to 40%, with identity/deduplication defined before enrollment so repeated conversions or minor variants cannot defeat the limit. With 10 scores the maximum is four. Report unseen compositions. |
| Accuracy | No attributable input above its governing reservation (margin R−U at least zero); unresolved exposure or missing required attribution cannot establish a clean pass. Retain exact margins, evidence status, headroom and per-source distribution. Zero is a screen for observed misses, not a safety bound. On the 16 historical eligible unchanged-image pairs the +426 to +519 figures were for the earlier flat anchor without an incremental proportional reserve. The first-trial formula yields +478 to +1,642 on those same pairs. A positive floor below the observed minimum would not reject them; neither set consists of decisive trial sends or supplies a justified tail floor. |
| Useful recovery | Require five distinct scored decisive opportunities with a reproducible exact-state full-local alternative and a completed evidence chain, preferably spread over at least two source families. For a projected deferral, identify the particular body, range or pixels retained. For a projected compaction selection, identify specific normal-context evidence made available and an associated executed/reconciled action; do not claim that the alternative would have completed a compaction or omitted a particular source forever. Verify affected pages in saved, reopened and exported output. Count an opportunity once regardless of how many links or pages it supplies. Mere page overlap, a proposed tool call or a fit flag alone does not qualify. Report adverse outcomes, vetoes, interruptions and whole-task fees where known separately. |

The minimum score count is a learning target for bounded expansion. Under fixed, independent and completely observed zero-failure sampling, ten attempts would give a one-sided 95% upper limit of 25.89%. Ten scored successes alone do not establish that denominator, and correlated conversions and early stops limit that model. A 20-slot trial spending eleven slots on alternate both-fit sends could score at most nine distinct decisive requests even if all twenty attempts succeed. Close insufficient evidence as inconclusive. A verified safety or product breach fails its applicable criterion. Meeting the full predeclared package only qualifies the exact profile for **separate review** of a bounded expansion with the same safeguards. No numbers have been adopted by this review; question 8 and the later design consolidation remain pending. [Promotion scope](#question-7-bounded-expansion-and-two-sided-acceptance-2026-11-05).

### Question 7 recommended final-payload exclusion and source families (2026-11-05)

**Recommendation to the user.** Accept the preceding numerical package for a learning trial, with the both-fit alternate excluded. This is the recommended revision to the earlier funded-both-fit decision. The reviewer's endorsement and this recommendation do not constitute the user's acceptance of the exposure thresholds. Implementation and enrollment would in any case require separate authorization.

**Exact change in trial behavior.** When the original full-local-feedback payload (Pf) fits only under the eligible anchor, consider the anchored-feedback alternate (Pa). Authorize trial dispatch of Pa only if its anchor reservation fits and its full-local reservation exceeds the allowance on those same finalized bytes, subject to all other eligibility and dispatch gates. Otherwise discard Pa and return to ordinary full-local handling of Pf. If both fit Pa, count a discarded both-fit alternate, zero trial attempts and zero scored sends; this is not a veto or suspension. Ordinary handling may defer, prepare compaction, wait or stop. It must not simply send the previously blocked Pf. Other branches, including a governing veto on Pf with the enrolled C override or A hold, are unchanged. Preserve the physical attempt budget and slot checks at dispatch, including permitted retries. No speculative evaluation or ordinary C send spends a trial attempt.

All 16 prior scalar cases were reclassified offline. Only the previously dispatchable both-fit alternate loses dispatch eligibility. In its native fixture Pf's full-local and anchored reservations were 7,169 and 7,159 at an allowance of 7,168; Pa's were 7,163 and 7,154. Rejecting Pa deliberately gives up an otherwise locally fitting payload. A one-token original shortfall says nothing about the cost of the resulting fallback or how often it occurs. Record the discarded reason, the actual ordinary disposition and any subsequent delivery impact. This protects the finite attempt budget from alternates that cannot meet the decisive criterion. It cannot guarantee more scores or a smaller probability of an inconclusive result, since taking the ordinary path can change later history.

Under this revision, anchored feedback goes out only on trial-funded same-byte decisive dispatches. A dispatch is not necessarily scored: acceptance and attributable input are still required. Even when scored, admission, delivered content and displayed feedback vary together. Record associated agent actions but do not attribute them causally to displayed headroom. Showing anchored feedback on additional classes of requests in a future expansion changes the intervention.

**Enrollment identity.** Identify a document by the SHA-256 of the immutable source PDF bytes behind the task's source reference. Record a stable family ID for the underlying work at enrollment, with a human declaration or confirmed document-as-its-own-family default. Known editions, re-scans, OCR versions and re-exports share one family. Reuse the recorded family for repeat enrollments of the same hash. Distinct hashes alone do not establish independence. Retain original declarations. If a relationship is discovered later, record a linked correction and re-evaluate the spread gate before an expansion decision. Do not silently relabel the cohort after scoring or use automated similarity as an authority. [Source import](../../src/pdfmd/evidence.py)

Apply the minimum of four **scored document hashes** and both 40% caps separately to all distinct scored logical requests, with exact integer comparison and no rounding up. Four hashes do not imply four families. For ten scores on four conversions and four hashes, counts of 2+2 on two editions of one work and 3+3 on two other works pass spread (largest family 4). Counts of 3+2 on the related editions and 3+2 on the others fail (largest family 5), even though every conversion individually passes. These are spread tests only, not complete promotion results.

**Package submitted for product acceptance.** One slot; at most 20 trial-funded physical attempts; first specified adverse outcome stops exposure and first governing veto suspends; no more than ten enrolled conversions; closing horizon 60 days from activation, with prior closing and reconciliation semantics. At least ten distinct scored decisive sends across four source documents, no conversion or family over 40%, zero observed reservation exceedances, all margins and unresolved outcomes reported, and at least five distinct complete useful-recovery chains. Prefer usefulness across more than one family. Budget, cohort or clock closure with insufficient informative evidence is a legitimate inconclusive outcome. Meeting the complete package can earn only separate review for bounded expansion. The held-out fixture and spread checks are under `.local/trial-exclusion-audit-20261105-ecf91d4a/`. Question 8 remains last, with design consolidation following settlement. [Threshold details and evidence-chain definition](#question-7-first-trial-numbers-under-review-2026-11-05).

### Question 7 first-trial package accepted (2026-11-05)

The user accepted the specification, including exclusion of both-fit alternates. Question 7 is settled. This acceptance does not activate a trial or authorize implementation, provider calls or enrollment. It supersedes the earlier proposal that both-fit alternate sends consume a trial slot.

| Decision | Accepted rule |
| --- | --- |
| Authority and exposure | One outstanding slot, at most 20 trial-funded physical attempts including permitted retries. Send the anchored-feedback alternate only when its anchor fits and full-local blocks on the same final bytes. Discard a both-fit alternate, record its reason and follow ordinary handling of the original payload without spending trial budget or suspending. Stop further exposure on the first specified adverse outcome. The first governing veto suspends the trial, with C as default and A available at enrollment. |
| Close | Stop new exposure at the earliest applicable limit: budget exhaustion, closure of the cohort after ten distinct enrolled conversions, or 60 days from activation. The tenth enrollment closes intake. Zero-opportunity and early-terminated conversions count. Complete reconciliation and fidelity review separately. |
| Spread | At least ten distinct scored final-payload decisive logical requests with attributable input; at least four scored document hashes; at most 40% from one conversion and at most 40% from one declared source family. Apply both caps to the same denominator without rounding up. Freeze the source hash and family declaration at enrollment, preserve the original and reevaluate spread on any later linked family correction. |
| Accuracy and useful recovery | Zero observed reservation exceedances and full margin/outcome reporting. Missing or unresolved required evidence cannot establish a clean pass. At least five distinct complete chains linking specific included content and a reproducible full-local alternative to an executed or reconciled associated action and source-led checks on the saved, reopened and exported result. Association does not establish causation. |
| Outcome | Meeting all gates earns separate review for a still-bounded expansion. An established breach can reject the trial. Insufficient informative evidence yields a valid inconclusive close without automatic extension. Routine authority and wider concurrency need separate decisions. |

For n scored logical requests, any family or conversion can supply at most floor(2n/5). Two such groups cannot cover n. At n=10 the maximum is four, and at least three scored families are necessary throughout the feasible 10–20 range. In this app one conversion imports one source PDF, so the separate four-document minimum actually requires **at least four scored conversions**, drawn from at least three works. Four different hashes from a single book cannot pass. Each qualifying family needs to contribute scored boundary evidence. Yield is unknown; inconclusive is a realistic outcome, not a measured probability. Anchored feedback appears only on decisive dispatches, including any that never become scoreable. Content admission and reported headroom still change together. The cost of discarding an alternate requires observing the actual fallback disposition. [Enrollment and closing details](#question-7-first-trial-numbers-under-review-2026-11-05) and [source-family and decision-table checks](#question-7-recommended-final-payload-exclusion-and-source-families-2026-11-05).

### Question 8 native complete-input counting (2026-11-05)

**Documentation check.** Distinguish a gateway route in a public API catalogue, availability for a deployed provider/model, and equivalence to the exact request the app would send. None of these alone establishes configured or actual context capacity.

| Path | Evidence and implication |
| --- | --- |
| Direct OpenAI Responses | OpenAI documents `POST /v1/responses/input_tokens` for Responses-format inputs, including message structure, images/files and tools. This is an applicable preflight capability for a compatible native Responses input; it is a separate network operation. [Counting guide](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/token-counting) and [method reference](https://developers.openai.com/api/reference/resources/responses/subresources/input_tokens/methods/count). |
| Direct OpenAI Chat Completions | The reviewed Chat Completions reference does not document a native preflight counter for the full Chat request. Reported prompt usage is post-response evidence. Reserializing Chat messages, tools and image content as a Responses request counts a different protocol representation unless the serving route explicitly guarantees equivalence. [Chat Completions reference](https://developers.openai.com/api/reference/resources/chat/subresources/completions). Other compatible providers require their own contract. |
| Nexus | Msty lists Responses input counting and Anthropic Messages counting alongside Chat Completions. Its gateway documentation makes support dependent on provider/model and endpoint family. It does not document either counter as exact for the historical Chat Completions alias and payload. The project's historical runtime record identifies the gateway and a modified managed backend, with no route-specific Chat counting guarantee. Both selected runs used Chat Completions. Current deployment capability was not queried. [Nexus gateway routes](https://docs.msty.ai/nexus/gateway) and [saved route research](../research/20261031_0157_NEXUS_STREAMING_SIGNALS.md). |
| Current app | Route validation selects a Chat Completions or Responses generation endpoint. The assembler uses separate native field and tool shapes; the send path posts the frozen payload to its configured generation endpoint. No preflight counting operation is implemented. [Route schema](../../src/pdfmd/schema.py) and [native request assembly and dispatch](../../src/pdfmd/nexus.py). |

**Recommended first-specification boundary, for discussion.** Keep native complete-input counting optional and outside the initial bounded trial. Direct Chat Completions remains usable with the local estimate and eligible post-response usage. Revisit a counter only for a named route/protocol with a documented same-input contract covering the actual model, tools, image detail and any gateway transformations. Count only an authorized final input, with separate authority, latency, request/fee accounting and a visible unavailable path. Internal hypothetical projections still need local estimates. A remote count alone does not prove admission, delivery benefit, or the upstream limit. A shortcut that converts Chat to Responses would change the subject. No counting, capabilities, provider or probe endpoint was called. After the user settles this question, consolidate the accepted rules in the versioned [context-management design owner](../design/CONTEXT_MANAGEMENT.md#assembled-request-budget).

### Question 8 settlement and decisive-only future option (2026-11-05)

Remote counting is optional and excluded from the first trial. The historical and contemplated first route uses native Chat Completions. No applicable complete Chat-input counter is established for it. Direct Chat Completions requires neither Nexus nor a Responses conversion. The app also builds native Responses requests. For such a route the count service accepts Responses input semantics, including instructions, tools and image detail. Match the countable **input** of the finalized candidate. The count operation has its own schema; the generation envelope also carries controls such as streaming and the output limit. Identical HTTP bodies are not the criterion. Count results do not establish output allowance or upstream context capacity. [OpenAI counting guide](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/token-counting) and [count request schema](https://developers.openai.com/api/reference/resources/responses/subresources/input_tokens/methods/count).

**Future use, separately authorized.** Prefer a count only for a named native Responses candidate for which the anchor admits the final bytes and full-local would block. This avoids a count round trip on ordinary admissions. For Nexus, first consult an existing deployment contract if available. If separately authorized, one read of `GET /v1/capabilities` is the smallest gateway discovery step. Inspect its actual scope: a global endpoint listing alone cannot establish availability for the selected model/provider, nor whether counting sees the same resolved model, defaults and upstream transformations as dispatch. For direct OpenAI use the relevant Responses contract; the Nexus discovery path is specific to Nexus. Preserve the model/route version, original frozen candidate hash and the mapping to count-request fields. Establish coverage of all applicable input-shaping fields, tools and native images. The reviewed docs do not establish route-specific count fees, rate limits or equivalence for the historical proxy. [Nexus gateway and capability discovery](https://docs.msty.ai/nexus/gateway) and [app payload assembly](../../src/pdfmd/nexus.py).

A usable count below the configured input allowance (or equal to it) supplies an additional admission check, subject to the ordinary output reserve, image/protocol coverage, freshness, available slot, task budget and cancellation gates. A count above the allowance withholds the candidate. Unavailability, timeout or an unsupported field never grants dispatch. Follow the independently authorized fallback: the existing trial rules only if that future mode explicitly permits uncounted decisive sends; otherwise use ordinary full-local handling. Account for count requests, errors, latency and unknown fees separately from generation attempts. A preflight gate would withhold some requests that the trial otherwise sends, altering the observed population. Separate count-assisted outcomes from unassisted estimator evidence and retain usage reconciliation on actual sends. No count, discovery, provider or probe endpoint was contacted.

All eight questions are settled for the first specification. Its normative owner is [Usage anchoring v1](../design/CONTEXT_MANAGEMENT.md#usage-anchoring-v1-first-bounded-trial-2026-11-05). This discussion remains the review, decision and evidence history. Design adoption, implementation, enrollment and routine promotion are separate states.

### Pre-implementation amendments and missing-usage disposition (2026-11-05)

The design owner now carries the settled review findings on original pre-deferral Pf selection, failed Pa finalization, per-route/edit eligibility and projection, atomic veto/disposition, slot ownership through settlement, frozen comparison evidence and promotion denominators. The first-trial fit table and accepted numerical limits remain in force. Ten scored decisive requests and five useful cases cannot compensate for an additional decisive exposure whose required input usage remains unattributable. A dispatched and accepted request can be a successful conversion step and an unscorable trial exposure at the same time. [Amended specification](../design/CONTEXT_MANAGEMENT.md#usage-anchoring-v1-first-bounded-trial-2026-11-05)

**Accepted dispatch rule (2026-11-05).** The user accepted the following first-trial disposition. Pause new decisive dispatches for the named trial lineage immediately if, after normal local settlement and reconciliation, an accepted trial-funded request still has no attributable normalized input. A missing intermediate streaming usage field is not the trigger. Record acceptance, attempt and payload identities, the reason attribution failed and the consumed exposure. Publish the pause durably before making the slot available to another task. Preserve the accepted reply and authorized tool effects; ordinary full-local operation can continue within existing task limits. Do not retry the conversion to obtain a usage figure.

Continuing the trial would spend scarce exposure without knowing whether the first unscored send exceeded its reservation. Later scores cannot repair that missing comparison, so a numerical quorum cannot restore a clean pass. Pausing could interrupt a productive trial because of a transient accounting/reporting issue, but it need not stop the conversion. If retained evidence later supplies valid usage for the same attempt, reconcile it and require explicit review before resuming the same trial with its existing budget. If reconciliation establishes an exceedance, follow the settled stop rule. If required attribution remains unavailable, the trial cannot pass and may close inconclusively. Missing usage alone establishes neither overflow nor estimator failure. This decision applies to the specification. Implementation and enrollment require separate authorization.

### Deployed comparator and implementation order (2026-11-05)

The final review identified two full-local methods that could give different verdicts on identical bytes. On Nursing #27, the deployed reservation was 111,640 and the offline decoded full-flat reservation was 110,412. Both fit the actual allowance of 122,880. An allowance between those estimates illustrates why the method identity matters. The design now names the deployed enrollment-off method as F for ordinary decisions, both finalized payload comparisons and the same-state counterfactual. The decoded method remains available as a labeled shadow comparator. A changed baseline can change opportunity classification in either direction on a particular workload. Review carry-over before any further trial exposure. [Design owner](../design/CONTEXT_MANAGEMENT.md#eligibility-and-pricing)

Production calibration remains a separate decision. Across the two runs, decoding reduced the original excess of 844,092 by 29,974 tokens (3.55%). Of the remaining 814,118 tokens, the text reserve accounted for 252,393 (31.00%) and the fixed reserve for 34,816. The image-row remainder of 525,277 (64.52%) includes any text or protocol mismatch on those rows. Interpreting it entirely as image overcharge requires assuming those other errors are zero. The remaining image-free residual was 1,632. These are two runs on the same source. They do not select new production allowances.

The reviewer recommended establishing the deployed baseline before collecting evidence for trial authority. We recommend recording versioned shadow observations first and deciding the production profile explicitly before enrollment. An observer built earlier can be tested against today's baseline. A new baseline needs a fresh equivalence check and a review of which earlier observations can still be compared. A failed counter load can recover, some R01 repairs alter payloads, and a long image-heavy source can yield no decisive opportunities. Keep those considerations separate from both shadow implementation and live enrollment. The user selected preparation of implementation documentation for a separate conversation. Calibration, engineering repairs, operational observation and enrollment remain unselected. [Implementation handoff](../REVIEW_HANDOFF.md#usage-anchoring-implementation-handoff)

## R01-CAL study discussion and real-PDF validation (2026-11-06)

### Request, evidence and authority

Recorded 2026-11-06, Asia/Singapore (UTC+08:00), against application checkpoint `e69d462c304df09f1f3c33aa79f4e44f42d564ab`. The user requested discussion of study scope, development evidence, acceptance gates and independent validation, explicitly keeping the work offline and production calibration unchanged. This is the user's reported knowledge, not a filesystem inventory or proof that no such data exists. The later handoff authorizes these records; it does not adopt the proposal or execute a study.

The assistant inspected maintained records and evaluator/estimator source. No private task corpus, application run, calibration evaluation, provider/count/probe call or new independent review was performed. The [34-send population and provenance](#exact-population-and-provenance) and [100-profile development screen](#offline-joint-checks-completed-for-this-proposal) are inherited evidence: two conversions of one PDF, 32 normal requests and two compactions, all already inspected. Among the screened profiles with no observed undercount, `r=0, F=0, A=3072` had 20.44% aggregate excess, 22.35% on Run A, 4.30% on Run B and a smallest margin of only 18 tokens. No screened zero-undercount profile met the proposed 15% per-run target. These findings neither establish a safe profile nor rule out every possible uniform profile.

### Proposed bounded offline development study

The assistant recommends assessing the recorded Chat Completions route with its actual model alias, parameters, accounting and image-processing scope. Keep configured capacity and output reserve fixed; make no transfer claim for Responses, another provider or UTF-8 fallback. Retain complete native payloads, source language and every image occurrence. Use the existing evaluator family `E = T + ceil(r*T) + F + I*A`, with current complete-payload BPE proxy `T` and native occurrence count `I`. The allowances are joint reservation parameters, not separately measured text, protocol or image costs. [The adopted evaluator contract](../design/CONTEXT_MANAGEMENT.md#offline-calibration-evaluation-v1) and [engineering guide](../guides/CONTEXT_AND_EVIDENCE.md#offline-calibration-evaluator) remain authoritative for software behavior.

All 34 sends belong to development, including the compactions and image-heavy counterexamples. A selected execution would verify exact saved payloads and matching native usage through retained provenance, preserving original estimates separately from current recomputation. Missing evidence remains unavailable; unsupported or unknown accounting is not replaced by zero. The historical full normal payloads at the two pressure boundaries were not frozen and must not be invented. Synthetic controls establish mechanism behavior only, not provider-count accuracy.

Recompute current `T` and `I` once per payload to determine whether R01-MOD changes the historical measurements. If counts and arithmetic are unchanged, reuse the earlier grid results rather than repeat the search. The initial proposed diagnostic controls are:

| Control | Text margin r | Fixed F | Uniform image A | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Current baseline | 25% | 1024 | 4096 | Preserve the governing comparison. |
| Half text reserve | 12.5% | 1024 | 4096 | Isolate a text-reserve reduction. |
| Zero additional text reserve | 0% | 1024 | 4096 | Show the remaining image/proxy restriction. |
| Earlier best grid profile | 0% | 0 | 3072 | Recheck the joint in-sample result, without selecting it. |
| Image-halving counterexample | 25% | 1024 | 2048 | Retain the known undercount discriminator. |

Only changed measurements warrant reassessing the existing 100-profile screen. Keep each complete frozen Route: A and B have different waiting settings, and the evaluator fingerprints the full Route. Separate study inputs where full fingerprints differ can share candidate definitions and a combined assessment; do not relabel historical configuration or substitute the estimator's narrower identity. Actual paths, hashes and native attribution require a separately selected evidence-access scope. No study file, source list or candidate nomination exists from this discussion.

Joint assessment addresses the substantial image residual as well as text uncertainty. Text-only adjustment is a useful control but does not resolve the demonstrated composition dependence. Additional image classes, a changed text projection or broader fitting would need a new method/evidence discussion if the bounded screen fails. Do not silently relax gates, remove counterexamples or expand the search until something passes. A failed screen can complete the selected question without proving the entire family infeasible. Raw inputs/results would remain in a unique ignored `.local/<study>/` directory, with immutable inputs and exclusive new report files; shareable rationale/results belong in maintained owners.

### Proposed accuracy gates and separate admission claim

These remain assistant proposals for a particular study, not adopted product requirements or provider guarantees. The evaluator's existing mandatory evidence/undercount rules remain effective independently of choosing thresholds.

| Condition | Proposed study treatment |
| --- | --- |
| Undercount | Any observed undercount on retained attributable development or validation evidence fails the candidate. |
| Run accuracy | Positive weighted excess at most 15% on each independent validation run. |
| Composition accuracy | Positive weighted excess at most 25% on every required validation composition group with declared support. |
| Evidence/support | Missing required provenance, unavailable measurement, unknown validation usage or insufficient declared coverage prevents a clean pass. Retain development unknowns and every denominator. A numerical counterexample still fails when other evidence is incomplete. |
| Accounting | Report signed error, positive excess, worst/count of undercounts, known/unknown/unavailable rows, unique payloads and physical attempts separately. Retries and nested histories are not independent source support; unknown fees remain unknown. |
| Admission | Report exact same-payload fit/headroom changes separately. Numerical accuracy does not establish actual route capacity, useful admission, fidelity or whole-task savings. |

Positive weighted excess is `sum(max(E-U, 0)) / sum(U)` over scored observations; signed cancellation cannot hide undercounts or excess. The assistant proposes `require_fit_improvement=false` **for the numerical study only**. All 34 original sends already fitted; they cannot demonstrate a newly admitted request. The two unfrozen boundary calculations remain labeled sensitivity evidence. This explicitly refines the earlier combined numerical/admission proposal: numerical validation may finish while actual admission and product benefit remain open under R01. It does not change the evaluator contract or select a production policy. With no independent validation data, a development-only evaluation is inconclusive unless a counterexample already establishes failure; it must not be called validated.

### Independent validation proposal and subsequent real-PDF clarification

Initially the assistant suggested four distinct PDFs, four runs and 40 unique frozen payloads across at least three document families (text/table-heavy, scanned/OCR and mixed figure-heavy), with at least ten scored observations per run and five unique payloads per group, each group represented by two PDFs. Proposed groups were short/schema-heavy text, longer text-only normal requests, normal requests with 1-4 images, normal requests with 5+ images, and compactions; language mix and pressure-boundary coverage also needed declaration. These were engineering coverage suggestions, not statistical confidence bounds. The evaluator enforces some declared counts; semantic independence, distinct-source coverage and unique-payload support need an evidence audit. No such corpus was found or selected.

The user then asked whether converting real PDFs would test the product much better. The assistant agreed and **revised the recommendation to prioritize a small, deliberately varied set of complete real conversions and their source-led outcomes when live work is separately selected**. The four-PDF/40-payload figures and associated support counts remain provisional planning suggestions, not fixed prerequisites, request quotas or demonstrated optimal spending. Counts should describe coverage; complete requested conversions should drive the evaluation. This clarification does not adopt the numerical gates, authorize conversions, require unrelated findings to be cleared, or reopen the accepted Nursing task.

The two evidence questions have distinct completion conditions:

| Question | Suitable evidence and limit |
| --- | --- |
| Does the reservation match reported input? | Fresh conversions under unchanged production F can supply independent native payload/usage observations. Offline comparison on those exact bytes can validate a previously frozen candidate numerically, subject to honest provenance, scope and coverage. It does not show how changed calibration would alter the agent's decisions. |
| Does candidate calibration improve conversion? | Separately selected conversions using the candidate must check the source against complete saved/reopened/exported Markdown and images, autonomous progress, manual intervention, compaction/retrieval/repair overhead, runtime and price-weighted cost where measurable. A baseline comparison needs declared source/task/configuration conditions; different PDFs or scopes alone do not establish causal benefit. Full source access and fidelity remain unchanged. |

For independent numerical validation, freeze one nominated candidate, thresholds, inclusion rules and manifests before inspecting validation residuals. Keep whole sources/runs separate from development, including overlapping extracts or related editions, not merely different hashes. Record who inspected what and when; hash equality and `previously_inspected=false` declarations alone do not prove chronology or semantic independence. Do not choose among several candidates after seeing validation. Once validation informs a revision, it becomes development evidence and the revision needs a different untouched set. Until actual sources are identified, no predeclaration flag or invented source hash can supply missing independence.

Fresh real evidence would resolve questions that replaying the inspected single-PDF cohort cannot: transfer to other compositions and actual end-to-end behavior. Any live stage would first need selected sources/scope, route, finite cost/exposure allowance, evidence separation and stop/review conditions. A formal comparison converter receives no matching-range reference answer, another arm's output or developer transcription; reviewers can consult sources. This handoff selects no live stage, candidate activation, operational observer period or UA-01 enrollment. No historical allowance transfers to new work.

### Planned limits and stopping behavior

The user accepted this planning direction; these limits describe a future bounded interval, not a current live allowance. Run A first and review its result before B. A blocking execution/evidence defect stops the batch for diagnosis; an ordinary recoverable tool error can be handled autonomously within the same task allowance. No new task or Continue may reset consumed limits.

| Limit | Per task | Combined trial / enforcement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| App-recorded physical model sends | **12**, configured with `max_requests=12` | **24** across the two declared tasks. Normal, compaction and response-correction sends share the allowance. Unused A allowance does not increase B. |
| Known active execution | **1,200 seconds / 20 minutes**, `max_active_seconds=1200` | **2,400 seconds / 40 minutes** of known active time across tasks; app persistence/checks enforce the per-task budget. |
| Wall-time Stop threshold | **1,500 seconds / 25 minutes from each Start** | **3,000 seconds / 50 minutes** across the two Start-to-Stop intervals, not a clock that charges intervening offline review. Operator monitoring enforces the threshold and records actual Stop latency. |
| Starts and retries | One Start; `replay_unknown=false`, one attempt per request | Two Starts maximum; no automatic Continue/restart, replacement run, replay or allowance extension. Existing bounded response corrections share total budgets. |
| New monetary commitments | Existing Nexus subscription only | No additional purchase, top-up or paid fallback. This is not a claim of zero consumption cost or a verified dollar ceiling. |

The [runtime contract](../design/RUNTIME_AND_DELIVERY.md#shared-limits-and-transport-behavior) and [current task limits](../design/RUNTIME_AND_DELIVERY.md#existing-task-settings-and-active-time-accounting) distinguish durable send/known-active gates from remote work. The earlier two-page FD-01 result used seven sends and about four active minutes; it is a limited planning reference, not a guarantee that these harder scopes finish within twelve sends. All useful partial work survives a limit. Unknown crash tails, connector-internal retries, upstream overlap/completion and fees stay unknown. Local Stop does not prove remote cancellation or a strict billing bound. If the actual route can incur metered charges, establish its billing basis and a monetary ceiling before a separately selected live interval; do not turn request counts into an invented price estimate.

End further dispatch on user Stop, a budget/Stop threshold, terminal provider/runtime failure, acceptance/storage conflict or unknown remote/local outcome. Preserve partial/native evidence and do not launch a replacement to obtain a passing result. Do not inject interruptions into this trial. R03's unknown OCR/script continuation gap remains relevant; blind replay is not a remedy. The trial imposes no field preset, compulsory OCR/text-first/preview stage, arbitrary per-tool quota or forced compaction.

### Acceptance and interpretation

| Outcome | Planned discriminating check and judgment |
| --- | --- |
| Complete content | Compare every scoped source page/region with the selected saved, reopened and exported result. Check wording, numbers, units, symbols/negation, meaningful emphasis, reading order, cross-page paragraphs, quotations, footnotes and any tables present. No known required omission, fabrication or unresolved substantive ambiguity receives an unqualified pass. |
| Usable figures | For B, inspect all four figure units, complete original captions and adjacent searchable labels/values/units/panel relationships under [FD-01](../design/FIGURE_DELIVERY.md#required-outcome). Preserve faithful appearance and applicable original/source/page/region provenance. Check exported assets actually resolve; a full-page preview is not automatically a complete independent figure delivery. |
| Durable delivery | Identify the chosen immutable version; compare saved/reopened/exported content and assets. Record scope exclusions explicitly, especially page 3's later section and page 4's out-of-scope body. A ZIP creation or agent `complete` claim alone is insufficient. |
| Autonomy | No operator transcription, crop coordinates, matching reference answer or manual content repair. Record any intervention and its effect separately. Optional tool use/non-use is not itself a quality verdict. Reviewers can inspect sources; converters do not receive reviewer answers. |
| Calibration evidence | Preserve exact frozen payload/route/estimate, logical request and physical attempt, native response/usage and accepted correspondence. Retain failed, missing, unsupported and unknown observations in the denominators; never replace unknown input/cost with zero. Keep cached/reasoning subsets within their proper accounting categories. |
| Cost and useful progress | Report attempts by kind, active/wall time, compaction/retrieval/repair overhead, manual intervention and known/unknown usage/fees. Request/token reductions alone do not establish whole-task price-weighted savings. |

Report content as passed, failed or incomplete separately from usable/partial calibration evidence. A failed conversion can still supply valid numeric observations; readable output can lack usable usage attribution. Budget exhaustion or incomplete review closes the bounded attempt honestly rather than lowering fidelity or deleting difficult scope. No broad source-family acceptance or installed-distribution readiness follows from this pair.

### Independence, unresolved bindings and next work

News Writing was already used in the [Chinese text/OCR transfer investigation](#question-3-cross-split-trial-candidate-2026-11-05) and associated earlier text comparisons (see the same-book/different-representations discussion). It belongs to development evidence even though these conversion tasks would be new. The old 34 sends also remain development. Gynecology is not certified untouched merely because no calibration run was found in this bounded selection read. Before claiming any B validation, establish provenance and freeze one candidate, gates and inclusion rules before B starts and before its calibration results are inspected. Otherwise B is baseline/product or development evidence, with independent validation unresolved. No candidate has been nominated or frozen here.

Retain the candidate zero-observed-undercount check and report the earlier proposed 15% per-run/25% per-composition excess targets only with their declared scope/status. Sparse or absent groups remain evidence gaps; two sources do not establish broad route-wide calibration. There is no minimum request count, obligation to fill forty payloads or requirement to trigger compaction. The numerical-only `require_fit_improvement=false` proposal still leaves actual admission benefit open. A distinct later candidate-conversion comparison would be needed to establish changed-calibration benefit; production calibration remains unchanged in this plan.

Source paths/hashes, section boundaries and Low effort are now concrete. Actual current route/configuration and billing classification, the future task/output identities, operational readiness and a live execution selection remain to be bound. Keep credentials, private workspaces and raw outputs outside versioned records; do not recover credentials from logs. The next useful action is to finish offline readiness planning for this selected pair, or, when explicitly requested, prepare/run a bounded interval using these exact constraints. Current handoff authority covers records only; it starts no task/chat/subagent and selects no provider work, fitting, application change, packaging or publication. Existing R03, OBS-02/03 and distribution findings retain their separate dependencies and revisit conditions; they are not a universal preflight repair queue. ### Offline route, billing and operational readiness (2026-11-06)

**Authority and result.** Continuing from handoff `ce1d80f` on `codex/project-organization`, the user selected remaining offline planning, concrete unresolved bindings and preservation of the recorded delivery/usage checks. The sources, 6.1-sol Low choice, A-before-B review and 12-send / 1,200-known-active-second / 1,500-second-wall-Stop limits above remain selected planning constraints. Readiness assessment used tracked code and maintained evidence; selected PDF reads were limited to preservation hashes. This continuation accessed no private connection file, credential or task store, launched no app/task/study, and made no network/provider/count/probe call. No production calibration, settings, dependencies or application code changed. The following is a preparation plan, not a claim that the route is ready or a new live allowance.

**Concrete remaining bindings.** A non-secret effective configuration supplied by its owner can resolve local configuration facts without a generation probe. Keep the full private snapshot outside Git; versioned records need only the permitted identity, basis and remaining unknowns. Historical metadata and UI starter values are not current settings.

| Binding | Inspected basis and required completion evidence | Present status / consequence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Gateway and account route | Bind the full Nexus Chat Completions endpoint, alias `cliproxy/gpt-6.1-sol`, current Nexus/CLIProxyAPI versions, selected subscription account/tier and any routing, retry or paid-fallback policy. The [historical route investigation](../research/20261031_0157_NEXUS_STREAMING_SIGNALS.md) connected the alias to a local provider; its provider base URL is not a current full Nexus gateway endpoint. | Intended protocol/alias are concrete; current endpoint, account mapping and connector behavior remain unverified. Do not substitute the historical provider URL or a direct paid API. |
| Low effort and native capabilities | Bind the complete `parameters` object with `reasoning_effort: "low"`, image-input capability/`vision`, native tool round trips and terminal usage support. [Route validation](../../src/pdfmd/schema.py) accepts this non-reserved parameter and [make_payload](../../src/pdfmd/nexus.py) forwards it unchanged in Chat Completions; this proves application plumbing only. | Actual gateway/upstream handling of Low, tools, images and usage remains unverified. Use available configuration/documentation or prior attributable evidence first; report absent evidence honestly. Any mismatch needs resolution before dispatch; no silent model/effort/protocol fallback or separate paid test is selected. |
| Governing capacity and estimator | Record current `context_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `image_tokens`, `estimation_profile`, `vision` and production estimator/code identities. Preserve their effective values and full source access. Record all other native parameters too. | Values were not inspected. Do not fill them from UI defaults or claim configured capacity is the route's demonstrated limit (CTX-01). An unsuitable current profile is an unresolved binding, not permission to recalibrate or reduce scope. |
| Response waiting and correction policy | Record exact `wait_policy` and `timeout_seconds`, plus `max_response_repairs`. Present wait policy selects streaming; null/absent policy retains the legacy deadline. The [waiting contract](../design/STREAMING_RECEIVE.md#streaming-progress-waiting) defines first-progress, idle and optional total waits. | Current values remain unbound. Preserve the effective policy; do not silently migrate a legacy route to 600/300/null or infer a streaming total from the legacy field. Keep the existing bounded correction policy and count its sends within 12. Any proposed change must be explicit before preparation. |
| Billing and exposure | Establish whether this exact route is subscription-only with no metered spillover/fallback. Record dated account/plan evidence, quota behavior and unknown fee attribution. If metering is possible, bind currency, applicable input/cache/output/other charges, an explicit total monetary ceiling for A+B and its per-task allocation, and a practical enforcement method covering outstanding upstream work/retries. | Billing remains unverified; no price or monetary ceiling is selected. The app has request/time gates, not a monetary gate. A nominal ceiling without a workable control cannot establish bounded spend. Resolve this before live dispatch; no purchase, top-up or paid fallback is permitted. Public direct-provider prices and the alias do not establish the Nexus bill. |
| Execution and artifact identity | Bind the actual development source/lock identities, batch/store/connection paths, two fresh task IDs, scope instructions, effective route/limits, Start/Stop records, selected immutable version IDs and new export destinations. | The namespace below is planned only; no directories/tasks/versions/exports have been created. Assign real identities during separately authorized preparation, not by inventing hashes or IDs now. |

The [offline evaluator](../guides/CONTEXT_AND_EVIDENCE.md#offline-calibration-evaluator) fingerprints the complete validated Route using `calibration.route_fingerprint(Route.model_validate(...))`, including defaults; this differs from the estimator's narrower identity. Preserve each request's actual frozen route and exact canonical payload bytes. Compare A/B effective configurations before execution; if they differ, retain separate fingerprints and explain the comparison limit rather than normalizing history. Low effort already distinguishes the intended configuration from the old 34 sends. No candidate or study is frozen by recording this plan.

**Local preparation route, still unexecuted.** Use the current development launcher, not `Start Workbench.cmd`, `-Packaged` or the old installer: those artifacts do not contain the current application baseline. A short future namespace is `workspaces/rc-<unique>/store`, with exports under `outputs/rc-<unique>/`; `<unique>` must be replaced once with a new batch identity. Retain that identity and actual absolute paths in private execution records. The [desktop host](../../src/desktop/main.ts) derives `connection.json` from the **parent of the store**, so this layout places it at `workspaces/rc-<unique>/connection.json`. Merely choosing `workspaces/rc-A` and `workspaces/rc-B` would still share `workspaces/connection.json`. Do not read, overwrite or copy an existing connection/secret as an isolation shortcut. Enter credentials through the normal secure connection flow when preparation is authorized. Workspace selection does not isolate Electron's default browser profile; use one app instance serially and bind the actual profile/instance.

Before a later Start, check the declared local environment without provider traffic: pinned dependencies/development launch, correct store, desktop source rendering/native-pixel access, saved-output preview and export destination support. Check OCR worker and language-data provisioning/cache availability without making OCR compulsory. A fresh store may need first-use language downloads; this planning's offline restriction is not a promise that future conversion has all language data locally. Leave trusted task scripts off unless a later process-level opt-in is explicitly selected. These checks are planned, not executed acceptance. Do not copy reviewer renders, reference-output files, notes or matching answers into converter input.

The desktop imports a PDF as an unconfigured task, then takes connection route/limits at its first Start; a configured task instead retains its saved settings. During separately authorized preparation, bind the fresh task's effective settings before any request is frozen, through the supported stopped-task settings path where needed. Verify `max_requests=12`, `max_active_seconds=1200`, `replay_unknown=false`, `max_attempts_per_request=1` and the recorded correction policy for **each** task. Connection defaults alone do not prove effective task settings. No limits/settings write occurs in this continuation.

**Operation and A-before-B review.** A future operator must be able to monitor the full interval and request Stop at 1,500 elapsed seconds from Start, or earlier on the recorded failure/budget conditions. Record Start, Stop-request and settled-stop times, known active seconds and unknown tails separately. The [runtime contract](../design/RUNTIME_AND_DELIVERY.md#active-time-measurement-and-unmeasured-intervals) and source checks show durable pre-send gates and wait polling; they do not establish an exact wall cutoff or instantaneous Stop during synchronous work. One Start is an operational constraint, not an app lifetime counter. No second click, Continue, replay, extension, replacement or budget reset follows from a stop/reopen. Export and factual review require no new inference.

Finish A's source-led saved/reopened/exported review and attempt/usage reconciliation before deciding whether B remains informative and operationally ready under the later live authority. An execution/storage/attribution defect that blocks interpretation stops the batch. A content failure remains a failure even if its numeric evidence is usable; it does not authorize repairing A or repeating it. Record the A-to-B disposition and any remaining defects. B's allowance remains 12 sends/1,200 known active seconds/1,500 wall seconds regardless of A's unused allowance. No automatic B launch is selected.

**Evidence and acceptance remain separate.** Preserve the complete checks in [Acceptance and interpretation](#acceptance-and-interpretation). For every physical attempt, retain kind, logical request/attempt IDs, frozen payload and route/estimate references/hashes, original response or SSE/assembled links, native usage, acceptance status and settled correspondence. Reconcile all attempts against the durable job count; distinguish unsent prepared inputs, normal/compaction/correction sends, rejected/unknown outcomes and any connector-internal retry uncertainty. The app's 12 sends count dispatch attempts at its own boundary, not demonstrably every upstream generation.

For CC, attributable inclusive input uses native `prompt_tokens`; cached details remain subsets, and output/reasoning is not added to input or charged twice. The [usage interpreter](../../src/pdfmd/usage.py) validates reported counts but does not certify provider accounting or fees. A later evaluator wrapper must bind run/attempt/protocol, request digest, complete route fingerprint, `accounting="inclusive_input_v1"` and exact native usage, with external provenance checked against the saved native/accepted evidence. Missing input, unsupported semantics and conflicting attribution remain unavailable/unknown; preserve every denominator and failed attempt. Do not replay merely to acquire usage. A blocking attribution defect stops progression to B; a missing field still remains evidence rather than zero or a reason to discard the run. Baseline product evidence can be reported separately when numerical interpretation is incomplete.

Review the actual selected immutable version in the reopened app and extracted export, including every scoped source region, page map, all B figure/caption/label relationships, portable asset links and retained originals/provenance. Short paths reduce exposure to R14; they do not prove destination acceptance. Preserve useful partial work and original files on failure. News Writing remains development; B remains baseline/product or development evidence unless provenance and a single candidate, gates and inclusion declaration are established before B starts and before inspection of its calibration results. Neither source is currently a certified holdout; no numerical candidate, 15%/25% gate or fit-improvement setting has been adopted.

**Selection and deferrals.** R01-CAL remains high-impact: existing evidence supports substantial avoidable restriction, and its magnitude uncertainty does not make it minor. This increment makes a selected inexpensive plan executable once its actual bindings and authority exist; it supplies no accuracy/savings result. R03 remains a substantial local-process interruption-recovery gap: no interruption is injected, and any unknown OCR/script outcome closes this interval without blind replay. Revisit recovery if it blocks the selected task. OBS-02/03 retain measured reconstruction/scan costs but have no selected workload/threshold and no observer is enabled here. UA-01-R1/R2 retain local repairs; UA-01-DEFAULTS concerns sparse offline coordinator construction, which this ordinary conversion plan does not use. R08/R14 require an actual supported export/viewer check at the declared destination; failure there promotes a scoped delivery remedy. Broader R01-PACK/DIST-01 packaging remains deferred to a selected build/distribution need. None is silently dismissed or turned into an unrelated preflight repair queue.

**Completion boundary.** Offline planning is complete at this scope with the table's bindings unresolved. Next obtain the permitted effective route/account/billing facts and bind local preparation/monitoring evidence; separately select live execution if desired. If independent numerical validation is intended, resolve its provenance and predeclaration before using B. Otherwise retain a baseline/development result without a holdout claim. No conversion, provider probe, fitting/evaluator run, operational observation, UA-01 enrollment, production change or publication is authorized by this record.
